From FMRP Project, CDUPS will query SSI Stamping service in RATAN for confirmation. Per discussed with POs (Settlement PO Dinesh/Confirmation PO Amol), requirement settled down per below:

1. Given trade and cashflow stamping must be sitting together, Reiterate the necessity of having central SSI stamping service and all are aligned.
2. [@Ghorpade, Amol](mailto:Amol.Ghorpade@sc.com) [@Ahamed, Fayaz](mailto:Fayaz.Ahamed@sc.com) confirmed that the notification on vostro refresh is required for CDUPS based on confirmation client (ad-hoc, call based) request. 1. SSI Vostro refresh (not publish to CDUPS) 2. Ratan Nostro refresh (not publish to CDUPS) 3. Settlement Ops ad-hoc SSI stamping (not publish to CDUPS, may required only on CDUPS call based) 4. Fixing Notice - will response to CDUPS with latest cashflow SSI stamping result, prior to general SSI stamping result 5. Trade event - CDUPS will make a call again. 6. There maybe scenario that post trade SSI stamping, cashflow SSI stamp with different SSI, so CDUPS would query the latest cashflow SSI on call basis.
3. There will be linkage between trade SSI stamping and cashflow SSI stamping, but no inherit for cashflow SSI stamping from trade SSI stamping, which means cashflow stamping process will remain the same.
4. For trade SSI stamping:

## Error Code

| Code | Code | Error Details | CDUPS Action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 200 | Success/DEFAULT_NOSTRO | | |
| 400 | VALIDITION_FAILED/ Trade not found | Effective Date Missing, Settlement Means Missing, Settlement Account Missing, .../Trade id + Major Version can't be found | |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | Unexpected exception captured, please contact support team with this trackingId for further investigation. | Retry the transaction posting using the same trade id + major version, with minimum 3 times at 3 minutes interval. |
| | Ratan no response/Timeout | | Retry the transaction posting using the same trade id + major version, with minimum 3 times at 3 minutes interval. |

# Below are old model based on SCBML

~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

The inbond trade SCBML template would be different cross products.

- Currencies: FX Spot/Forward would have 2 Currencies Buy & Sell while FX Swap there're 2 currency pairs
- Buy/Sell identifier: the xpath to identify the buy/sell currency would be different.

The outbond( API response) would be different as well for different products.

This document would capture the templates supported in trade SSI stamping service.

General Vostro and Nostro query input:

| | Field | Physical Model |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Vostro Query Condition | Branch FM Code | Query SCI according to Legal Entity FMID |
| Counterpart FMID | /scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:party[@id="party2"]/conf:partyId[@partyIdScheme="[http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/partyId/FMID](http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/partyId/FMID)"] |
| Currency | Different logic in different products |
| CFI Code | (/scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:trade|/scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/((*/(*:originalTrade|*:trade))|((*:novation|*:cancelReissue)/*:newTrade)))/*/conf:productType[@productTypeScheme="[http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/external/product-classification/financialInstrumentCode](http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/external/product-classification/financialInstrumentCode)"] |
| Settlement Method | (/scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:trade|/scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/((*/(*:originalTrade|*:trade))|((*:novation|*:cancelReissue)/*:newTrade)))/conf:tradeHeader/conf:partyTradeInformation/scbextn:settlementMethod[@settlementMethodScheme="[http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/settlementMethod](http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/settlementMethod)"] |
| Settlement Type | Hardcode as "CASH" |
| Debit/Credit | Different logic in different products |
| SSI Status(Active/Inactive) | |
| Nostro Query Condition | Legal Entity FMID | /scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:party[@id="party1"]/conf:partyId[@partyIdScheme="[http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/partyId/FMID](http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/partyId/FMID)"] |
| Payment Currency | Different logic in different products |
| Settlement Means | From Vostro |
| Settlement Account | From Vostro |

#### CFI Code

Common logic updated (Story [6500808](https://dev.azure.com/sc-ado/FMQPR/_workitems/edit/6500808)):

Step1: Get initial SSI

spot/forward/swap - hardcord *F**** 
IRS/CCS/Bullion Spot/forward/swap/MTM CCS/NDIRS/NDCCS - from SCBML
fixing - from API

Step2 Refactor CFI code with * for sequence 213456
Query SSI for all CFI code and it parent CFI Code, i.e. SF**** would query CFI code include (SF****, *F***, ******)

Stamp settlementInstruction according best matching rule : If Vostro exist for CFI code from trade SCBML, then pick this Vostro, else pick Vostro for parent CFI Code, else pick Vostro for '******'

#### Settlement Method (Aligned with cashflow SSI stamping)

| **Settlement Method from Trade** | **Settlement Method to query Vostro** |
| --- | --- |
| CASH | (CASH,FEDWIRE) |
| GROSS | (CASH,FEDWIRE) |
| Other Value | (Other Value, CASH,FEDWIRE) |

#### Effective Date

- Update the effective date query logic

#### Best matching logic for trade is the same with cashflow

# FX Spot/Forward

Nostro/Vostro Query Fields

| Field | Physical Model |
| --- | --- |
| Currency 1 | /scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:trade/scbextn:fxSingleLeg/conf:exchangedCurrency1/conf:paymentAmount/conf:currency[@currencyScheme="[http://www.fpml.org/coding-scheme/external/iso4217-2001-08-15](http://www.fpml.org/coding-scheme/external/iso4217-2001-08-15)"] |
| Currency 2 | /scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:trade/scbextn:fxSingleLeg/conf:exchangedCurrency2/conf:paymentAmount/conf:currency[@currencyScheme="[http://www.fpml.org/coding-scheme/external/iso4217-2001-08-15](http://www.fpml.org/coding-scheme/external/iso4217-2001-08-15)"] |
| Debit/Credit | For CCY 1 If /scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:trade/scbextn:fxSingleLeg/conf:exchangedCurrency1/conf:payerPartyReference/@href == 'Party1' Then "Credit" Else 'Debit' For CCY 2 If /scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:trade/scbextn:fxSingleLeg/conf:exchangedCurrency2/conf:payerPartyReference/@href == 'Party1' Then "Credit" Else 'Debit' |

Outbound enrichment

| Sequence | Currency | Data Source | Field enriched to CDUPS |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | SCB Buy/Debit Currency | SCB Nostro | - Field 53: correspondentInformation |
| 2 | SCB Sell/Credit Currency | Client Vostro SCB Nostro | - Field 58 : beneficiary - Field 56: intermediaryInformation - Field 57: correspondentInformation - Field 53: account |

| Field | Physical Model |
| --- | --- |
| Tracking ID | /scb:SCBML/scb:header/scb:originationDetails/scb:trackingId |
| Trade ID | (/scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/conf:trade|/scb:SCBML/scb:payload/scb:FPMLPayload/((*/(*:originalTrade|*:trade))|((*:novation|*:cancelReissue)/*:newTrade)))/conf:tradeHeader/conf:partyTradeIdentifier[conf:partyReference/@href="party1"]/conf:versionedTradeId/conf:tradeId[@tradeIdScheme="[http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/tradeId/tradeLake](http://www.sc.com/coding-scheme/tradeId/tradeLake)"] |

If the value doesn't exist in SCBML/API, can be skipped, which won't impact trade SSI stamping
